7,'#0b2232'],[1,'#526977']]),'#b6a17b',3);c.restore();
 rounded(c,x-83,y-93,161,174,10,'#0b1d2bc9','#bf9f68b3',2);for(const [xx,yy] of [[x-87,y-100],[x+81,y-100],[x-87,y+92],[x+81,y+92]])rivet(c,xx,yy,4);

function pendulum(c,x0,y0,L,a,t,refAngle=0){const bx=x0+L*Math.sin(a),by=y0+L*Math.cos(a);
 // ghost baseline arc and real motion mark, both geometric not arbitrary effects
 c.save();c.strokeStyle='#c9e2ea4e';c.setLineDash([4,9]);c.lineWidth=2;c.beginPath();c.arc(x0,y0,L,Math.PI/2-Math.max(.17,refAngle),Math.PI/2+Math.max(.17,refAngle));c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);c.restore();
 illumination(c,bx,by,68,'#f4d89e',.12);weight(c,x0,y0,bx,by,29);apparatusMount(c,x0,y0);
 return [bx,by];}

};
mobileBossClock=function(c,t){const W=c.canvas.width,H=c.canvas.height,b=clockBoss;begin(c,'tower','時計塔の最終修理',t);

 bottomHud(c,W,402,title,desc,hot||done);

/* Furiko Quest v0.15 — cinematic interaction-aware art and observation system.
   Does not change the underlying physics, success criteria or saved progress. */
function glassSheen(c,x,y,w,h,t,intensity=.25){
 c.save();c.beginPath();c.roundRect(x,y,w,h,12);c.clip();
 const offset=((t*.16)%1.65)-.45;
 const sh=c.createLinearGradient(x+w*offset,y,x+w*(offset+.55),y+h);
 sh.addColorStop(0,'#ffffff00');sh.addColorStop(.33,`rgba(240,255,248,${intensity*.10})`);
 sh.addColorStop(.49,`rgba(247,243,208,${intensity*.8})`);
 sh.addColorStop(.53,`rgba(255,255,255,${intensity*.17})`);sh.addColorStop(.8,'#ffffff00');
 c.fillStyle=sh;c.fillRect(x,y,w,h);c.restore();
}
function etchedDial(c,x,y,r,angle,progress,legend){
 c.save();c.shadowColor='#061018';c.shadowBlur=15;c.shadowOffsetY=4;
 disk(c,x,y,r,rgba(c,x-r,y-r,x+r,y+r,[[0,'#d5b87c'],[.1,'#6a634e'],[.52,'#223643'],[1,'#927453']]),'#e2c690',2);c.restore();
 disk(c,x,y,r-8,rgba(c,x,y-r,x,y+r,[[0,'#173342'],[.6,'#0a1e2b'],[1,'#183846']]),'#ba9f70',2);
 for(let i=0;i<=12;i++){const a=-Math.PI*.8+i*Math.PI*1.6/12;const long=i%3===0;
  c.strokeStyle=long?'#e6d9b4':'#9bbaa7';c.lineWidth=long?2:1;
  c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+Math.cos(a)*(r-14),y+Math.sin(a)*(r-14));c.lineTo(x+Math.cos(a)*(r-(long?25:19)),y+Math.sin(a)*(r-(long?25:19)));c.stroke();}
 const a=-Math.PI*.8+Math.PI*1.6*Math.max(0,Math.min(1,progress));
 c.save();c.shadowColor='#9af5cf';c.shadowBlur=10;c.strokeStyle='#b8f7df';c.lineWidth=2.6;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x-Math.cos(a)*9,y-Math.sin(a)*9);c.lineTo(x+Math.cos(a)*(r-26),y+Math.sin(a)*(r-26));c.stroke();c.restore();
 disk(c,x,y,5,'#f1d7a0','#203c48',1.2);
 text(c,legend,x,y+r+19,13,'#f5e3c1','center');
}
function tinyEngraving(c,x,y,copy,w=98){
 rounded(c,x,y,w,24,5,'#091c2cd9','#baaa8350',1);

function metalDust(c,t,x1,y1,w,h,amount=13,color='#ffe8bb'){
 if(reduceMotion())return;
 c.save();for(let i=0;i<amount;i++){
  const p=randomAt(104+i*17),q=randomAt(19+i*47),x=x1+p*w+Math.sin(t*.2+i*12)*3,y=y1+((q+(t*(.01+.005*(i%3))))%1)*h;
  c.globalAlpha=.10+(.15+.16*Math.sin(t*.9+i)*Math.sin(t*.9+i))*(i%5===0?1:.5);
  disk(c,x,y,i%4===0?1.5:.7,color);
 }c.restore();
}
function specularSweep(c,x,y,r,t,active){
 if(!active)return;
 const s=reduceMotion()?0:t;
 c.save();c.translate(x,y);c.rotate(s*.36);
 const g=c.createLinearGradient(-r,-r,r,r);
 g.addColorStop(0,'#ffffff00');g.addColorStop(.36,'#ffffff00');g.addColorStop(.5,'#ffefbd62');g.addColorStop(.59,'#ffffff05');g.addColorStop(1,'#ffffff00');
 c.fillStyle=g;c.beginPath();c.arc(0,0,r,0,TWO);c.fill();c.restore();
}
function glint(c,x,y,age,color='#ffe9b2'){
 if(age<0||age>1||reduceMotion())return;
 const grow=1+Math.sin(Math.PI*age)*1.6;
 c.save();c.globalAlpha=(1-age)*.9;c.translate(x,y);c.strokeStyle=color;c.shadowColor=color;c.shadowBlur=13;c.lineWidth=2;
 for(const xy of [[-20,0,20,0],[0,-20,0,20]]){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(xy[0]*grow,xy[1]*grow);c.lineTo(xy[2]*grow,xy[3]*grow);c.stroke();}c.restore();
}
